+describe('cross-Listing price sharing with a user-owned row (A23, Req 6/7/14/15)', () => {
+  let server: TestServer;
+  let sourceId: string;
+  // The suite owns its DuckDB directory (rather than letting the helper
+  // create and delete one) because the middle test below reads and writes
+  // the file directly, which means stopping the server first: DuckDB holds
+  // an exclusive lock on the file while the server process has it open.
+  let dataDir: string;
+  let serverRunning = false;
+  let listingAId: string;
+
+  // Boots the server against this suite's own data directory and
+  // (re-)creates the Source every Listing below hangs off. Called once up
+  // front and again after the middle test's direct DB access, which has to
+  // close the server to take the file lock.
+  async function startServer(): Promise<void> {
+    server = await startTestServer(TEST_PORT, { IDENTIFY_PARTS_POOL_SIZE: '0', PRICE_PARTS_POOL_SIZE: '0', DUCKDB_DATA_DIR: dataDir });
+    serverRunning = true;
+  }
+
+  beforeAll(async () => {
+    dataDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'pc-parts-flipper-cross-listing-'));
+    process.env.DUCKDB_DATA_DIR = dataDir;
+    // Exception (ts-no-dynamic-import): client.ts resolves DUCKDB_DATA_DIR
+    // once at module-evaluation time, so it has to be loaded after the env
+    // var points at this suite's directory — the same reason
+    // priceSourceRoute.test.ts imports it this way.
+    const { getConnection, closeConnection } = (await import('../src/db/client.js')) as typeof ClientModule;
+    await runMigrations(await getConnection());
+    // Single-writer rule (client.ts's own module doc): this connection must
+    // be closed before the server process opens the same file.
+    await closeConnection();
+
+    await startServer();
+    const sourceRes = await postJson<SourceResponse>(`${server.baseUrl}/api/sources`, {
+      name: 'Cross-listing price sharing source',
+      platformType: 'ebay',
+      url: 'https://example.com/cross-listing',
+      notes: null,
+    });
+    expect(sourceRes.status, `seed Source creation failed: ${JSON.stringify(sourceRes.body)}`).toBe(201);
+    sourceId = sourceRes.body.id;
+  }, 120_000);
+
+  afterAll(async () => {
+    if (serverRunning) {
+      await server.stop();
+    }
+    await rm(dataDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
+  }, 30_000);
+
+  // A23, half 1 (the write): the only row this Listing holds for the combo
+  // is user-owned and unpriced — the state a user lands in by deleting an
+  // auto-filled price. The row itself must stay 'manual' with no value.
+  it('stores Listing A\'s user-owned row verbatim: manual, no price', async () => {
+    const created = await postJson<ListingDetail>(
+      `${server.baseUrl}/api/listings`,

+        {
+          componentCategory: SHARED_CATEGORY,
+          brand: SHARED_BRAND,
+          partNumber: SHARED_PART,
+          description: 'Card, price deleted by the user',
+          estimatedResaleValue: null,
+          identityCandidates: [],
+          aiConfidence: null,
+          aiSource: null,
+          priceSource: 'manual',
+        },
+      ]),
+    );
+
+    expect(created.status, `create failed: ${JSON.stringify(created.body)}`).toBe(201);
+    listingAId = created.body.id;
+    expect(created.body.expectedParts[0].priceSource).toBe('manual');
+    expect(created.body.expectedParts[0].estimatedResaleValue).toBeNull();
+
+    // Re-fetched, not echoed: the response body is built from the rows as
+    // inserted, while triggerPriceLookups runs afterwards inside the same
+    // request, so only a fresh read proves the trigger left this row alone.
+    const fetched = await getJson<ListingDetail>(`${server.baseUrl}/api/listings/${listingAId}`);
+    expect(fetched.body.expectedParts[0].priceSource).toBe('manual');
+    expect(fetched.body.expectedParts[0].estimatedResaleValue).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  /**
+   * A23, half 1 (the shared effect), and the reason pricePartsTrigger's
+   * eligibility filter deliberately ignores `price_source`: Listing A's
+   * save must still have seeded the shared cache row (Req 6, 9) and queued
+   * the research job (Req 5) for the combo, even though the only row that
+   * triggered it belongs to the user and will never receive the result.
+   * That research is for every *other* Listing holding this part.
+   *
+   * Proven by reading the server's own DuckDB file, which means stopping
+   * the server first (exclusive file lock). The same connection then
+   * simulates the research completing — the worker pool is disabled here,
+   * and a real `omp` call has no place in a deterministic test — before the
+   * server is restarted for the final test.
+   */
+  it('still seeds the shared cache row and queues research for the combo (A23, Req 5/6/9)', async () => {
+    await server.stop();
+    serverRunning = false;
+
+    process.env.DUCKDB_DATA_DIR = dataDir;
+    // Exception (ts-no-dynamic-import): same module-evaluation-time reason
+    // as beforeAll above.
+    const { getConnection, closeConnection } = (await import('../src/db/client.js')) as typeof ClientModule;
+    const connection = await getConnection();
+
+    const cache = await connection.runAndReadAll(
+      `SELECT estimated_resale_value FROM part_price_cache
+       WHERE component_category = $category AND brand = $brand AND part_number = $part_number`,
+      { category: SHARED_CATEGORY, brand: SHARED_BRAND, part_number: SHARED_PART },
+    );
+    const cacheRows = cache.getRowObjectsJson();
+    expect(cacheRows, 'Listing A\'s save should have seeded exactly one shared cache row').toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(cacheRows[0].estimated_resale_value, 'the seeded row is unpriced until research runs').toBeNull();
+
+    const jobs = await connection.runAndReadAll(
+      `SELECT stage FROM price_parts_jobs
+       WHERE component_category = $category AND brand = $brand AND part_number = $part_number`,
+      { category: SHARED_CATEGORY, brand: SHARED_BRAND, part_number: SHARED_PART },
+    );
+    const jobRows = jobs.getRowObjectsJson();
+    expect(jobRows, 'Listing A\'s save should have queued exactly one research job').toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(jobRows[0].stage, 'research is queued, never run inside the request (Req 5)').toBe('queued');

+    // Research completes, the way the price worker pool would finish it:
+    // the cache entry gains a value, the job reaches a terminal stage. 412
+    // appears in no request body this suite sends, so it can only ever
+    // reach an Expected Part via the cache propagation.
+    await connection.run(
+      `UPDATE part_price_cache SET estimated_resale_value = 412, last_researched_at = current_timestamp
+       WHERE component_category = $category AND brand = $brand AND part_number = $part_number`,
+      { category: SHARED_CATEGORY, brand: SHARED_BRAND, part_number: SHARED_PART },
+    );
+    await connection.run(
+      `UPDATE price_parts_jobs SET stage = 'completed'

+      { category: SHARED_CATEGORY, brand: SHARED_BRAND, part_number: SHARED_PART },
+    );

+  }, 150_000);

+  // Listing A's 'manual' row for that same combo, which the very same

+          aiConfidence: null,
+          aiSource: null,

+    expect(createdB.status, `create failed: ${JSON.stringify(createdB.body)}`).toBe(201);
+    expect(createdB.body.expectedParts[0].priceSource).toBe('auto');
+
+    // Re-fetched: the propagation runs after the response body is built.
+    const fetchedB = await getJson<ListingDetail>(`${server.baseUrl}/api/listings/${createdB.body.id}`);
+    expect(fetchedB.body.expectedParts[0].estimatedResaleValue, 'Listing B should have received the cached price').toBe(412);
+    expect(fetchedB.body.expectedParts[0].priceSource).toBe('auto');
+
+    const fetchedA = await getJson<ListingDetail>(`${server.baseUrl}/api/listings/${listingAId}`);
+    expect(fetchedA.body.expectedParts[0].estimatedResaleValue, 'Listing A\'s user-owned row must stay empty').toBeNull();
+    expect(fetchedA.body.expectedParts[0].priceSource).toBe('manual');
+  }, 30_000);

    +// T4 proof: `price_source` threaded end to end through the Listing write
+// paths (part-price-estimation feature, Req 14-15), updated by T13 for the
+// three-state field (assumption A22, correcting A8): null = undecided,
+// 'auto' = the background pipeline owns the price, 'manual' = the user
+// does. Runs against the real server (helpers.ts's spawn-based fixture,
+// same as listings.test.ts) so what is proven is the whole chain — request
+// validation, the route's own 'auto' decision, the INSERT, and the column
+// coming back out on GET — rather than any one layer's in-memory behaviour.
+//
+// IDENTIFY_PARTS_POOL_SIZE=0 and PRICE_PARTS_POOL_SIZE=0 keep both worker
+// pools from claiming anything and invoking the real `omp` binary (same
+// reason identifyPartsRoute.test.ts sets the first), so every job these
+// writes enqueue simply sits queued — exactly what these assertions want.
+import type { DuckDBConnection } from '@duckdb/node-api';
+import type { CreateListingInput, ExpectedPart, ListingDetail } from '@pc-parts-flipper/shared';
+import type * as ClientModule from '../src/db/client.js';
+import { mkdtemp, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+import { runMigrations } from '../src/db/migrate.js';
+import { getJson, postJson, startTestServer, type JsonResponse, type TestServer } from './helpers.js';
+
+// Unique port so this suite can run alongside every other server-spawning
+// suite (highest currently in use is 34_635) without a collision.
+const TEST_PORT = 34_640;
+
+// The combo the "user clears an auto-priced value" regression owns. Its
+// cache row is seeded with a researched value before the server boots, so
+// the only thing that can ever put that number on an Expected Part is the
+// cache propagation — never this suite's own request bodies.
+const CLEARED_AUTO_PART = 'PS-CLEARED-AUTO';
+const CLEARED_AUTO_CACHED_VALUE = 275;
+
+interface SourceResponse {
+  id: string;
+}
+
+interface ErrorResponse {
+  error: { message: string; statusCode: number };
+}
+
+// PUTs `body` as JSON to `url`. helpers.ts has no PUT wrapper (see
+// listings.test.ts's identical local copy and its note on why).
+async function putJson<T>(url: string, body: unknown): Promise<JsonResponse<T>> {
+  const res = await fetch(url, { method: 'PUT', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body) });
+  return { status: res.status, body: (await res.json()) as T };
+}
+
+function listingBody(sourceId: string, expectedParts: CreateListingInput['expectedParts']): CreateListingInput {
+  return {
+    sourceId,
+    title: 'Price source threading rig',
+    description: null,
+    listingUrl: 'https://example.com/listing/price-source',
+    askingPrice: 200,
+    listingDate: '2026-02-01',
+    acquisitionFees: 0,
+    expectedParts,
+  };
+}
+
+// Named part numbers per test keep the price-cache/job rows these writes
+// create from one test being read by another.
+let partCounter = 0;
+function uniquePartNumber(): string {
+  partCounter += 1;
+  return `PS-TEST-${partCounter}`;
+}
+
+describe('price_source threading through the Listing write paths (T4)', () => {
+  let server: TestServer;
+  let sourceId: string;
+  // The suite owns its DuckDB directory (rather than letting the helper
+  // create and delete one) because the last test below reads the file
+  // directly after shutting the server down — see its own comment.
+  let dataDir: string;
+  let serverStopped = false;
+
+  beforeAll(async () => {
+    dataDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'pc-parts-flipper-price-source-'));
+    process.env.DUCKDB_DATA_DIR = dataDir;
+    // Exception (ts-no-dynamic-import): client.ts resolves DUCKDB_DATA_DIR
+    // once at module-evaluation time, so it has to be loaded after the env
+    // var points at this suite's directory — the same reason
+    // pricePartsConcurrency.test.ts imports it this way.
+    const { getConnection, closeConnection } = (await import('../src/db/client.js')) as typeof ClientModule;
+    const seedConnection = await getConnection();
+    await runMigrations(seedConnection);
+    // Written with raw SQL rather than through partPriceCache.ts so this
+    // fixture does not depend on the code the assertions judge.
+    await seedConnection.run(
+      `INSERT INTO part_price_cache (id, component_category, brand, part_number, estimated_resale_value, last_researched_at)
+       VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), 'GPU', 'NVIDIA', $part_number, $value, current_timestamp)`,
+      { part_number: CLEARED_AUTO_PART, value: CLEARED_AUTO_CACHED_VALUE },
+    );
+    // Single-writer rule (client.ts's own module doc): this connection must
+    // be closed before the server process opens the same file.
+    await closeConnection();
+
+    server = await startTestServer(TEST_PORT, { IDENTIFY_PARTS_POOL_SIZE: '0', PRICE_PARTS_POOL_SIZE: '0', DUCKDB_DATA_DIR: dataDir });
+    const sourceRes = await postJson<SourceResponse>(`${server.baseUrl}/api/sources`, {
+      name: 'Price Source Test Source',
+      platformType: 'ebay',
+      url: 'https://example.com/price-source',
+      notes: null,
+    });
+    expect(sourceRes.status, `seed Source creation failed: ${JSON.stringify(sourceRes.body)}`).toBe(201);
+    sourceId = sourceRes.body.id;
+  }, 95_000);
+
+  afterAll(async () => {
+    if (!serverStopped) {
+      await server.stop();
+    }
+    await rm(dataDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
+  }, 30_000);
+
+  it('claims a fully-identified, unpriced part for the pipeline (priceSource auto) and persists it', async () => {
+    const partNumber = uniquePartNumber();
+    const created = await postJson<ListingDetail>(
+      `${server.baseUrl}/api/listings`,
+      listingBody(sourceId, [
+        { componentCategory: 'GPU', brand: 'NVIDIA', partNumber, description: 'Card', estimatedResaleValue: null, identityCandidates: [], aiConfidence: null, aiSource: null },
+      ]),
+    );
+
+    expect(created.status, `create failed: ${JSON.stringify(created.body)}`).toBe(201);
+    expect(created.body.expectedParts[0].priceSource).toBe('auto');
+
+    // Re-fetched, not just echoed: proves the column was actually written.
+    const fetched = await getJson<ListingDetail>(`${server.baseUrl}/api/listings/${created.body.id}`);
+    expect(fetched.body.expectedParts[0].priceSource).toBe('auto');
+  });
+
+  // Req 14: a figure the user typed is theirs. Claiming it for the pipeline
+  // would let a later cache refresh overwrite it.
+  it('leaves a part the user already priced unclaimed (priceSource null)', async () => {
+    const partNumber = uniquePartNumber();
+    const created = await postJson<ListingDetail>(
+      `${server.baseUrl}/api/listings`,
+      listingBody(sourceId, [
+        { componentCategory: 'GPU', brand: 'NVIDIA', partNumber, description: 'Card', estimatedResaleValue: 310, identityCandidates: [], aiConfidence: null, aiSource: null },
+      ]),
+    );
+
+    expect(created.status, `create failed: ${JSON.stringify(created.body)}`).toBe(201);
+    expect(created.body.expectedParts[0].priceSource).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it('leaves a half-identified part unclaimed, since it can never be researched', async () => {
+    const created = await postJson<ListingDetail>(
+      `${server.baseUrl}/api/listings`,
+      listingBody(sourceId, [
+        { componentCategory: 'Storage', brand: 'Samsung', partNumber: null, description: '512GB NVMe', estimatedResaleValue: null, identityCandidates: [], aiConfidence: null, aiSource: null },
+        { componentCategory: 'RAM', brand: null, partNumber: 'DDR4-3200', description: '16GB', estimatedResaleValue: null, identityCandidates: [], aiConfidence: null, aiSource: null },
+      ]),
+    );
+
+    expect(created.status).toBe(201);
+    expect(created.body.expectedParts.every((part) => part.priceSource === null)).toBe(true);
+  });
+
+  // Req 14 across a resave. The editor sends back whatever priceSource the
+  // row currently holds unless the user edited it, so an auto row that has
+  // since been given its researched value must stay auto — recomputing from
+  // "has a price now" alone would silently hand it back to the user and
+  // stop every future refresh from reaching it.
+  it('keeps priceSource auto on a resave of an auto row that has since been priced', async () => {
+    const partNumber = uniquePartNumber();
+    const created = await postJson<ListingDetail>(
+      `${server.baseUrl}/api/listings`,
+      listingBody(sourceId, [
+        { componentCategory: 'CPU', brand: 'Intel', partNumber, description: 'Chip', estimatedResaleValue: null, identityCandidates: [], aiConfidence: null, aiSource: null },
+      ]),
+    );
+    expect(created.body.expectedParts[0].priceSource).toBe('auto');
+
+    const resaved = await putJson<ListingDetail>(
+      `${server.baseUrl}/api/listings/${created.body.id}`,
+      listingBody(sourceId, [
+        { componentCategory: 'CPU', brand: 'Intel', partNumber, description: 'Chip', estimatedResaleValue: 180, identityCandidates: [], aiConfidence: null, aiSource: null, priceSource: 'auto' },
+      ]),
+    );
+
+    expect(resaved.status, `update failed: ${JSON.stringify(resaved.body)}`).toBe(200);
+    expect(resaved.body.expectedParts[0].priceSource).toBe('auto');
+    expect(resaved.body.expectedParts[0].estimatedResaleValue).toBe(180);
+  });
+
+  // Req 15: the editor clears priceSource the moment the user touches a
+  // row, and the server must take that clearing at face value.
+  it('keeps priceSource null on a resave of a row the user has claimed', async () => {
+    const partNumber = uniquePartNumber();
+    const created = await postJson<ListingDetail>(
+      `${server.baseUrl}/api/listings`,
+      listingBody(sourceId, [
+        { componentCategory: 'CPU', brand: 'Intel', partNumber, description: 'Chip', estimatedResaleValue: 95, identityCandidates: [], aiConfidence: null, aiSource: null },
+      ]),
+    );
+
+    const resaved = await putJson<ListingDetail>(
+      `${server.baseUrl}/api/listings/${created.body.id}`,
+      listingBody(sourceId, [
+        { componentCategory: 'CPU', brand: 'Intel', partNumber, description: 'Chip, lid scuffed', estimatedResaleValue: 95, identityCandidates: [], aiConfidence: null, aiSource: null, priceSource: null },
+      ]),
+    );
+
+    expect(resaved.status).toBe(200);
+    expect(resaved.body.expectedParts[0].priceSource).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  // THE regression this three-state field exists for (Req 14/15, assumption
+  // A22). A row the pipeline auto-priced from the shared cache, whose value
+  // the user then deletes, comes back from the editor as brand + partNumber
+  // present, estimatedResaleValue null, priceSource 'manual'. On the old
+  // two-value field that was indistinguishable from a never-claimed row: the
+  // route re-claimed it as 'auto', and triggerPriceLookups then wrote the
+  // still-cached value straight back onto it inside the same request,
+  // silently undoing the deletion the user had just made.
+  it('never re-populates a cleared auto-priced value from the cache (Req 14/15)', async () => {
+    const created = await postJson<ListingDetail>(
+      `${server.baseUrl}/api/listings`,
+      listingBody(sourceId, [
+        { componentCategory: 'GPU', brand: 'NVIDIA', partNumber: CLEARED_AUTO_PART, description: 'Card', estimatedResaleValue: null, identityCandidates: [], aiConfidence: null, aiSource: null },
+      ]),
+    );
+    expect(created.status, `create failed: ${JSON.stringify(created.body)}`).toBe(201);
+    expect(created.body.expectedParts[0].priceSource).toBe('auto');
+
+    // The pre-seeded cache value lands on the row during that same save, so
+    // what follows is a genuinely auto-priced row, not a hypothetical one.
+    const priced = await getJson<ListingDetail>(`${server.baseUrl}/api/listings/${created.body.id}`);
+    expect(priced.body.expectedParts[0].estimatedResaleValue).toBe(CLEARED_AUTO_CACHED_VALUE);
+    expect(priced.body.expectedParts[0].priceSource).toBe('auto');
+
+    // The user deletes that price. The editor hands ownership over with it.
+    const cleared = await putJson<ListingDetail>(
+      `${server.baseUrl}/api/listings/${created.body.id}`,
+      listingBody(sourceId, [
+        { componentCategory: 'GPU', brand: 'NVIDIA', partNumber: CLEARED_AUTO_PART, description: 'Card', estimatedResaleValue: null, identityCandidates: [], aiConfidence: null, aiSource: null, priceSource: 'manual' },
+      ]),
+    );
+    expect(cleared.status, `update failed: ${JSON.stringify(cleared.body)}`).toBe(200);
+    expect(cleared.body.expectedParts[0].priceSource).toBe('manual');
+    expect(cleared.body.expectedParts[0].estimatedResaleValue).toBeNull();
+
+    // Re-fetched, not echoed: the response body is built from the rows as
+    // inserted, while the cache propagation runs afterwards in the same
+    // request, so only a fresh read can prove it left this row alone.
+    const refetched = await getJson<ListingDetail>(`${server.baseUrl}/api/listings/${created.body.id}`);
+    expect(refetched.body.expectedParts[0].priceSource).toBe('manual');
+    expect(refetched.body.expectedParts[0].estimatedResaleValue).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  // The same claim made on a combo the server has never seen: 'manual' is
+  // stored verbatim, so the row is never auto-claimed in the first place.
+  it('stores an incoming manual claim verbatim on a fully-identified, unpriced row', async () => {
+    const partNumber = uniquePartNumber();
+    const created = await postJson<ListingDetail>(
+      `${server.baseUrl}/api/listings`,
+      listingBody(sourceId, [
+        { componentCategory: 'GPU', brand: 'ASUS', partNumber, description: 'Card', estimatedResaleValue: null, identityCandidates: [], aiConfidence: null, aiSource: null, priceSource: 'manual' },
+      ]),
+    );
+
+    expect(created.status, `create failed: ${JSON.stringify(created.body)}`).toBe(201);
+    expect(created.body.expectedParts[0].priceSource).toBe('manual');
+
+    const fetched = await getJson<ListingDetail>(`${server.baseUrl}/api/listings/${created.body.id}`);
+    expect(fetched.body.expectedParts[0].priceSource).toBe('manual');
+  });
+
+  it('rejects an unrecognized priceSource value with a field-named 400', async () => {
+    const res = await postJson<ErrorResponse>(
+      `${server.baseUrl}/api/listings`,
+      listingBody(sourceId, [
+        // Exactly three states exist (assumption A22): 'auto', 'manual' and
+        // null. Anything else is a client bug, not a fourth meaning.
+        { componentCategory: 'CPU', brand: 'Intel', partNumber: uniquePartNumber(), description: 'Chip', estimatedResaleValue: null, identityCandidates: [], aiConfidence: null, aiSource: null, priceSource: 'cached' } as unknown as ExpectedPart,
+      ] as unknown as CreateListingInput['expectedParts']),
+    );
+
+    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
+    expect(res.body.error.message).toContain('expectedParts[0].priceSource');
+  });
+
+  /**
+   * Req 4 + Req 5, end to end: the save itself must leave a *queued*
+   * background job behind for the newly-claimed part — queued, not already
+   * researched, because the research never runs inside the request.
+   *
+   * Proven by reading the server's own DuckDB file, which means stopping
+   * the server first: DuckDB takes an exclusive lock on the file while the
+   * server holds it open, so a second connection from this process cannot
+   * be opened alongside it. Runs last for that reason, and sets
+   * `serverStopped` so afterAll doesn't wait forever on an exit event that
+   * already fired.
+   */
+  it('leaves a queued background price job behind, and no synchronous research (Req 4, 5)', async () => {
+    const partNumber = uniquePartNumber();
+    const created = await postJson<ListingDetail>(
+      `${server.baseUrl}/api/listings`,
+      listingBody(sourceId, [
+        { componentCategory: 'PSU', brand: 'Seasonic', partNumber, description: 'Focus 750W', estimatedResaleValue: null, identityCandidates: [], aiConfidence: null, aiSource: null },
+      ]),
+    );
+    expect(created.status, `create failed: ${JSON.stringify(created.body)}`).toBe(201);
+
+    await server.stop();
+    serverStopped = true;
+
+    process.env.DUCKDB_DATA_DIR = dataDir;
+    // Exception (ts-no-dynamic-import): client.ts computes its DATA_DIR once
+    // at module-evaluation time from DUCKDB_DATA_DIR, so it has to be
+    // imported after that env var points at this suite's directory — the
+    // same reason every direct-DB suite in this folder imports it this way.
+    const { getConnection } = await import('../src/db/client.js');
+    const connection: DuckDBConnection = await getConnection();
+
+    const jobs = await connection.runAndReadAll(
+      `SELECT stage FROM price_parts_jobs WHERE brand = $brand AND part_number = $part_number`,
+      { brand: 'Seasonic', part_number: partNumber },
+    );
+    const jobRows = jobs.getRowObjectsJson();
+    expect(jobRows).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(jobRows[0].stage).toBe('queued');
+
+    // Req 9: the cache row is inserted straight away, still unpriced, so the
+    // daily refresh finds the combo even if this job never completes.
+    const cache = await connection.runAndReadAll(
+      `SELECT estimated_resale_value FROM part_price_cache WHERE brand = $brand AND part_number = $part_number`,
+      { brand: 'Seasonic', part_number: partNumber },
+    );
+    const cacheRows = cache.getRowObjectsJson();
+    expect(cacheRows).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(cacheRows[0].estimated_resale_value).toBeNull();
+
+    delete process.env.DUCKDB_DATA_DIR;
+  }, 30_000);
+});

    +// @vitest-environment jsdom
+//
+// Proof for the `priceSource` threading through the editor (Req 14-15),
+// updated by T13 for the three-state field (assumption A22, correcting A8):
+// undefined/null = undecided, 'auto' = the background pipeline owns the
+// price, 'manual' = the user does. A cached, auto-priced value must survive
+// being round-tripped through the editor untouched, but the instant the
+// user edits any field on that row it must become 'manual' — not merely
+// cleared, which would read as undecided and let the server re-claim the
+// row (and re-fill the price the user had just deleted) on the next save.
+//
+// Part 1 (expectedPartToRow, via listing-form-state.ts) needs no React
+// mounting — it's a plain data-transform function, matching
+// listing-form-state.test.ts's own aiConfidence/aiSource precedent.
+// Part 2 (updateRow's clear-on-edit) mounts the real ExpectedPartsEditor
+// through a tiny stateful harness, matching expected-parts-editor.test.tsx's
+// "clears on edit" pattern for the AI-suggested badge, adapted for
+// priceSource. Part 3 (persistListing's outgoing payload) mounts the real
+// ListingForm and submits it, matching identify-parts.test.tsx's own
+// `onSubmit.mock.calls[0][0]` payload-inspection pattern.
+import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
+import { createRoot, type Root } from 'react-dom/client';
+import { act, useState } from 'react';
+import type { CreateListingInput, ExpectedPart, ListingDetail, Source } from '@pc-parts-flipper/shared';
+import { initialExpectedPartRows } from '../src/features/listings/listing-form-state';
+import { ExpectedPartsEditor, type ExpectedPartRow, type ExpectedPartRowErrors } from '../src/features/listings/expected-parts-editor';
+import { ListingForm } from '../src/features/listings/listing-form';
+
+globalThis.IS_REACT_ACT_ENVIRONMENT = true;
+
+const fetchLatestIdentifyPartsJobMock = vi.fn();
+
+// ListingForm mounts IdentifyPartsButton, which calls
+// fetchLatestIdentifyPartsJob on mount to resume any non-terminal job —
+// mocked here (resolving to null, no job to resume) purely so Part 3's
+// mount doesn't hit the real network, matching identify-parts.test.tsx's
+// own mocking of this module.
+vi.mock('@/features/listings/listings-api', () => ({
+  enqueueIdentifyPartsJob: vi.fn(),
+  fetchIdentifyPartsJob: vi.fn(),
+  fetchLatestIdentifyPartsJob: (...args: unknown[]) => fetchLatestIdentifyPartsJobMock(...args),
+  fetchListing: vi.fn(),
+  ackIdentifyPartsJob: vi.fn(),
+  uploadListingPhotos: vi.fn(),
+}));

+function makeExpectedPart(overrides: Partial<ExpectedPart> = {}): ExpectedPart {
+  return {
+    id: 'part-1',
+    listingId: 'listing-1',
+    componentCategory: 'GPU',
+    brand: 'NVIDIA',
+    partNumber: 'RTX 3070',
+    description: 'pulled from a working rig',
+    estimatedResaleValue: 300,
+    identityCandidates: [],
+    createdAt: '2024-01-01',
+    updatedAt: '2024-01-01',
+    ...overrides,
+  };
+}

+function makeListingDetail(expectedParts: ExpectedPart[]): ListingDetail {
+  return {
+    id: 'listing-1',
+    sourceId: 'source-1',
+    title: 'Test Listing',
+    description: null,
+    listingUrl: null,
+    askingPrice: 100,
+    listingDate: '2024-01-01',
+    status: 'open',
+    acquisitionFees: 0,
+    photoPaths: [],
+    externalSource: null,
+    externalItemId: null,
+    createdAt: '2024-01-01',
+    updatedAt: '2024-01-01',
+    comparison: { totalExpectedResaleValue: 300, totalCost: 100, difference: 200 },
+    expectedParts,
+  };

+describe('expectedPartToRow reads priceSource off the persisted part', () => {
+  it('carries priceSource: "auto" onto the row for a part the cache auto-priced', () => {
+    const rows = initialExpectedPartRows(makeListingDetail([makeExpectedPart({ priceSource: 'auto' })]));
+    expect(rows[0].priceSource).toBe('auto');
+  });
+
+  // Without this, a row the user took over would reopen as undecided and be
+  // re-claimed by the server on the very next save — the A22 bug, reached
+  // through a reload rather than through a single edit.
+  it('carries priceSource: "manual" onto the row for a part the user has taken over', () => {
+    const rows = initialExpectedPartRows(makeListingDetail([makeExpectedPart({ priceSource: 'manual' })]));
+    expect(rows[0].priceSource).toBe('manual');
+  });
+
+  it('leaves priceSource undefined for a part with no priceSource claim', () => {
+    const rows = initialExpectedPartRows(makeListingDetail([makeExpectedPart({ priceSource: null })]));
+    expect(rows[0].priceSource).toBeUndefined();
+  });
+
+  it('leaves priceSource undefined for a part that omits the field entirely', () => {
+    const rows = initialExpectedPartRows(makeListingDetail([makeExpectedPart()]));
+    expect(rows[0].priceSource).toBeUndefined();
+  });

+// Part 2: updateRow hands an auto row's price to the user on any edit.
+let container: HTMLDivElement | null = null;
+let root: Root | null = null;
+
+afterEach(() => {
+  act(() => {
+    root?.unmount();
+  });
+  container?.remove();
+  container = null;
+  root = null;
+});
+
+async function mount(element: React.ReactElement): Promise<HTMLDivElement> {
+  container = document.createElement('div');
+  document.body.appendChild(container);
+  await act(async () => {
+    root = createRoot(container!);
+    root.render(element);
+  });
+  return container;
+}
+
+function setValue(input: HTMLInputElement | HTMLSelectElement, value: string) {
+  const setter = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(window.HTMLInputElement.prototype, 'value')?.set;
+  if (input instanceof HTMLInputElement && setter) {
+    setter.call(input, value);
+  } else {
+    input.value = value;
+  }
+  input.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
+  input.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
+}
+
+function inputsLabelled(el: HTMLElement, suffix: string): HTMLInputElement[] {
+  return (Array.from(el.querySelectorAll('input')) as HTMLInputElement[]).filter((input) => input.getAttribute('aria-label')?.endsWith(suffix));
+}
+
+// Holds the rows in state so the controlled editor's onChange actually
+// round-trips, exactly as ListingForm's own `setRows` does. Also renders
+// each row's current `priceSource` into a plain-text <output> element —
+// ExpectedPartsEditor itself renders no visible priceSource indicator (no
+// badge, unlike aiSuggested), so this is the only way a test can observe
+// updateRow's clearing of it directly, rather than inferring it indirectly
+// through a DOM input's own value.
+function EditorHarness({ initialRows, rowErrors = [] }: { initialRows: ExpectedPartRow[]; rowErrors?: ExpectedPartRowErrors[] }) {
+  const [rows, setRows] = useState<ExpectedPartRow[]>(initialRows);
+  return (
+    <div>
+      <ExpectedPartsEditor rows={rows} rowErrors={rowErrors} onChange={setRows} />
+      <output data-testid="price-sources">{rows.map((row) => row.priceSource ?? 'none').join(',')}</output>
+    </div>
+  );
+}
+
+function autoPricedRow(overrides: Partial<ExpectedPartRow> = {}): ExpectedPartRow {
+  return {
+    componentCategory: 'GPU',
+    brand: 'NVIDIA',
+    partNumber: 'RTX 3070',
+    description: 'pulled from a working rig',
+    estimatedResaleValue: '300',
+    identityCandidates: [],
+    priceSource: 'auto',
+    ...overrides,
+  };
+}
+
+describe('ExpectedPartsEditor updateRow flips priceSource to manual on edit', () => {
+  it('flips priceSource from auto to manual the moment the row description is edited', async () => {
+    const el = await mount(<EditorHarness initialRows={[autoPricedRow()]} />);
+    const descriptionInput = inputsLabelled(el, 'description')[0];
+
+    expect(el.querySelector('output')!.textContent).toBe('auto');
+    await act(async () => setValue(descriptionInput, 'slightly scuffed shroud'));
+
+    expect(inputsLabelled(el, 'description')[0].value).toBe('slightly scuffed shroud');
+    expect(el.querySelector('output')!.textContent).toBe('manual');
+  });
+
+  it('flips priceSource to manual when the row category is edited instead of its text', async () => {
+    const el = await mount(<EditorHarness initialRows={[autoPricedRow()]} />);
+    const select = el.querySelector('select') as HTMLSelectElement;
+
+    await act(async () => setValue(select, 'Motherboard'));
+
+    expect(select.value).toBe('Motherboard');
+    expect(el.querySelector('output')!.textContent).toBe('manual');
+  });
+
+  // The case the whole three-state field exists for: deleting the
+  // auto-filled price is itself an edit, so the row becomes the user's. If
+  // this cleared to undecided instead, the next save would re-claim the row
+  // and the server would put the cached price straight back.
+  it('flips priceSource to manual when the user clears the auto-filled price itself', async () => {
+    const el = await mount(<EditorHarness initialRows={[autoPricedRow()]} />);
+    const valueInput = inputsLabelled(el, 'estimated resale value')[0];
+
+    await act(async () => setValue(valueInput, ''));
+
+    expect(inputsLabelled(el, 'estimated resale value')[0].value).toBe('');
+    expect(el.querySelector('output')!.textContent).toBe('manual');
+  });
+
+  // The rule is specifically "an auto row becomes the user's". A row that
+  // was never auto-managed has nothing to take over, so editing it must not
+  // invent a claim out of an undecided row.
+  it('leaves an undecided row undecided when it is edited', async () => {
+    const el = await mount(<EditorHarness initialRows={[autoPricedRow({ priceSource: undefined })]} />);
+    const descriptionInput = inputsLabelled(el, 'description')[0];
+
+    expect(el.querySelector('output')!.textContent).toBe('none');
+    await act(async () => setValue(descriptionInput, 'edited'));
+
+    expect(inputsLabelled(el, 'description')[0].value).toBe('edited');
+    expect(el.querySelector('output')!.textContent).toBe('none');
+  });
+
+  it('leaves an already-manual row manual when it is edited again', async () => {
+    const el = await mount(<EditorHarness initialRows={[autoPricedRow({ priceSource: 'manual' })]} />);
+    const descriptionInput = inputsLabelled(el, 'description')[0];
+
+    await act(async () => setValue(descriptionInput, 'edited again'));
+
+    expect(inputsLabelled(el, 'description')[0].value).toBe('edited again');
+    expect(el.querySelector('output')!.textContent).toBe('manual');
+  });
+
+  // MF1 regression: a price the user types himself (a row that was never
+  // 'auto') and then clears must not silently fall back to 'undecided' —
+  // that would let the server re-claim and re-fill it from the shared
+  // cache on the very next save, identically to the never-claimed case.
+  it('flips a never-auto row to manual when its price is typed then cleared', async () => {
+    const el = await mount(<EditorHarness initialRows={[autoPricedRow({ priceSource: undefined, estimatedResaleValue: '' })]} />);
+    const valueInput = inputsLabelled(el, 'estimated resale value')[0];
+
+    expect(el.querySelector('output')!.textContent).toBe('none');
+    await act(async () => setValue(valueInput, '250'));
+    await act(async () => setValue(inputsLabelled(el, 'estimated resale value')[0], ''));
+
+    expect(inputsLabelled(el, 'estimated resale value')[0].value).toBe('');
+    expect(el.querySelector('output')!.textContent).toBe('manual');
+  });
+
+  it('leaves a second, untouched auto-priced row alone when only the first is edited', async () => {
+    const el = await mount(<EditorHarness initialRows={[autoPricedRow(), autoPricedRow({ componentCategory: 'PSU', brand: 'Corsair', partNumber: 'RM750', description: 'boxed' })]} />);
+    const descriptionInputs = inputsLabelled(el, 'description');
+
+    expect(el.querySelector('output')!.textContent).toBe('auto,auto');
+    await act(async () => setValue(descriptionInputs[0], 'edited'));
+
+    expect(descriptionInputs[0].value).toBe('edited');
+    expect(inputsLabelled(el, 'description')[1].value).toBe('boxed');
+    // Row identity matters: only index 0 changed hands, index 1 is still 'auto'.
+    expect(el.querySelector('output')!.textContent).toBe('manual,auto');
+  });
+});
+
+// Part 3: persistListing's outgoing payload (mounted ListingForm, real submit).
+const sources: Source[] = [
+  { id: 'source-1', name: 'Test Source', platformType: 'ebay', url: null, notes: null, active: true, createdAt: '2024-01-01', updatedAt: '2024-01-01' },
+];
+
+function listingWithParts(expectedParts: ExpectedPart[]): ListingDetail {
+  return {
+    id: 'listing-1',
+    sourceId: 'source-1',
+    title: 'Gaming PC bundle',
+    description: 'Ryzen build',
+    listingUrl: 'https://example.com/item/1',
+    askingPrice: 400,
+    listingDate: '2024-01-01',
+    status: 'open',
+    acquisitionFees: 0,
+    photoPaths: [],
+    externalSource: null,
+    externalItemId: null,
+    createdAt: '2024-01-01',
+    updatedAt: '2024-01-01',
+    comparison: { totalExpectedResaleValue: 300, totalCost: 400, difference: -100 },
+    expectedParts,
+  };
+}
+
+beforeEach(() => {
+  fetchLatestIdentifyPartsJobMock.mockResolvedValue(null);
+});
+
+afterEach(() => {
+  fetchLatestIdentifyPartsJobMock.mockReset();
+});
+
+describe('ListingForm persistListing sends priceSource for Expected Parts', () => {
+  it('sends priceSource: "auto" for an untouched auto-priced row', async () => {
+    const listing = listingWithParts([makeExpectedPart({ priceSource: 'auto' })]);
+    const onSubmit = vi.fn(async () => listing);
+    const el = await mount(<ListingForm initialValue={listing} sources={sources} onSubmit={onSubmit} onCancel={() => {}} onSaved={() => {}} />);
+
+    const form = el.querySelector('form') as HTMLFormElement;
+    await act(async () => form.requestSubmit());
+
+    expect(onSubmit).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+    const payload = onSubmit.mock.calls[0][0] as CreateListingInput;
+    expect(payload.expectedParts[0].priceSource).toBe('auto');
+  });
+
+  // The wire-level half of the A22 fix: the edited row must go out as an
+  // explicit 'manual' claim. Sending null here would tell the server
+  // "undecided", and the server would re-claim the row for the pipeline.
+  it('sends priceSource: "manual" for an auto-priced row the user has since edited', async () => {
+    const listing = listingWithParts([makeExpectedPart({ priceSource: 'auto' })]);
+    const onSubmit = vi.fn(async () => listing);
+    const el = await mount(<ListingForm initialValue={listing} sources={sources} onSubmit={onSubmit} onCancel={() => {}} onSaved={() => {}} />);
+
+    const brandInput = inputsLabelled(el, 'brand')[0];
+    await act(async () => setValue(brandInput, 'ASUS'));
+
+    const form = el.querySelector('form') as HTMLFormElement;
+    await act(async () => form.requestSubmit());
+
+    expect(onSubmit).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+    const payload = onSubmit.mock.calls[0][0] as CreateListingInput;
+    expect(payload.expectedParts[0].priceSource).toBe('manual');
+  });
+
+  // Reopening a Listing whose row the user took over in an earlier session:
+  // the claim must survive the round trip, not decay back to undecided.
+  it('sends priceSource: "manual" for an untouched row the user had already taken over', async () => {
+    const listing = listingWithParts([makeExpectedPart({ priceSource: 'manual' })]);
+    const onSubmit = vi.fn(async () => listing);
+    const el = await mount(<ListingForm initialValue={listing} sources={sources} onSubmit={onSubmit} onCancel={() => {}} onSaved={() => {}} />);
+
+    const form = el.querySelector('form') as HTMLFormElement;
+    await act(async () => form.requestSubmit());
+
+    expect(onSubmit).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+    const payload = onSubmit.mock.calls[0][0] as CreateListingInput;
+    expect(payload.expectedParts[0].priceSource).toBe('manual');
+  });
+
+  it('sends priceSource: null for a row nobody has claimed', async () => {
+    const listing = listingWithParts([makeExpectedPart({ priceSource: null })]);
+    const onSubmit = vi.fn(async () => listing);
+    const el = await mount(<ListingForm initialValue={listing} sources={sources} onSubmit={onSubmit} onCancel={() => {}} onSaved={() => {}} />);
+
+    const form = el.querySelector('form') as HTMLFormElement;
+    await act(async () => form.requestSubmit());
+
+    expect(onSubmit).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+    const payload = onSubmit.mock.calls[0][0] as CreateListingInput;
+    expect(payload.expectedParts[0].priceSource).toBeNull();
+  });
+});
